[Bank 08]

[Table of Contents]
[2E00~2FFF: NPC Palette Indices]
[3000~3FFF: Exit Event Definitions]
[4000~43FE: Walkover Event Pointers]
[F000~FFFF: 16x16 Tileset Construction Data Pt. 3]

[20 4-byte patterns]
;	Loaded by a routine at C2/A6C9.
;	Seems to be Layer effects.
;	Byte 00: ??
;	Byte 01: Layer 1
;	Byte 02: Layer 2
;	Byte 03: ?? (Seems to relate the earthquake effects)
;	Ah, this is controlled by map header byte 4. AND the value with 1F to get an index into this table.
;	03 is used by the Tileset View Debug Map.
;	Not sure if any of the others are used at all.
C8/0000:	AA 00 00 00 [00: Default (Nothing)]
C8/0004:	AA 00 91 00 [01: Layer 2 Scrolls Diagonally SW]
C8/0008:	AA 00 0B 00 [02: Layer 2 Scrolls East]
C8/000C:	AA 00 B0 00 [03: Layer 2 Scrolls South]
C8/0010:	AA 00 30 00 [04: Layer 2 Scrolls North]
C8/0014:	0A 00 88 00 [05: Layer 2 Swirl Motion (Hard to describe...)]
C8/0018:	5A 00 00 00 [06: Layer 2 Follows Player]
C8/001C:	FA 00 22 00 [07: Layer 2 Scrolls Diagonally SE]
C8/0020:	AF 22 00 00 [08: Layer 1 Scrolls Diagonally SE]
C8/0024:	AF 28 00 00 [09: Layer 1 Swirl Motion]
C8/0028:	FA 00 00 00 [0A: Layer 2 Scrolls Opposite Player Movement]
C8/002C:	55 00 00 00 [0B: Layer 2 Follows Player]
C8/0030:	AA 00 04 00 [0C: Layer 2 Scrolls West]
C8/0034:	AA 00 0C 00 [0D: Layer 2 Scrolls East]
C8/0038:	0A 00 00 00 [0E: Layer 2 Follows Player]
C8/003C:	5A 00 09 00 [0F: Layer 2 Scrolls West (Slow)]
C8/0040:	5A 00 01 00 [10: Layer 2 Scrolls East (Slow)]
C8/0044:	5A 00 00 00 [11: Layer 2 Follows Player]
C8/0048:	AA 00 00 00 [12: Nothing]
C8/004C:	5A 00 00 00 [13: Layer 2 Follows Player]
C8/0050:	FF FF FF FF [14: Dummied Out]
C8/0054:	FF FF FF FF [15: Dummied Out]
C8/0058:	FF FF FF FF [16: Dummied Out]
C8/005C:	FF FF FF FF [17: Dummied Out]
C8/0060:	FF FF FF FF [18: Dummied Out]

[Map Display Settings]
;     Legend:
;     00:   Mosaic Effect              - $2106
;     01:   Main Screen Layers Enabled - $212C
;     02:   Sub Screen Layers Enabled  - $212D
;     03:   Color Math                 - $2131
;     04:   Unused Value 01            - Possibily Was $2126/$2128
;     05:   Unused Value 02            - Possibily Was $2127/$2129
;     06:   Unused Value 03            - Possibily Was $2101
;     0708: Background Palette
;     
;     Bytes 04, 05, and 06 are loaded but then completely ignored.
;     What purpose they would have served is unknown.
;     One of them was probably for the Object Size and Chr Address Register, as that value is now hardcoded.
;
;     Note that these values aren't modified at all, they are dumped directly into their respective registers.
;     The routine that loads this table is located at C2/AA60.

[Handled by C1/B0F6
Step 1: The number is subtracted from the next number; if 0, the result defaults to 2.
Step 2: That number is multiplied by 2 and used as read length.
Step 3: The number is then itself multiplied by 2 and used for an offset to the table at C8/0441.
]

{Handled by code at C1/E7CE; work through execution there when leaving a room/using an event, then change $DC~$E0.}
Rm: Room Number ($DC), destination of the exit. Takes D0 of X+ as a modifier.

X+: D0: figures into room number ($DD). The rest: Dunno, divided by 2 and stored into $DE (Apparently *= 10 later).
Tested in Potos Field 1.

#%0000 0001: ------------------------------------------------------- (D0: adds 0x100 to room number)
#%0000 0010: 7ee000  01 00 18 00 0a 00 00 00 00 00 00 03 00 00 00 00 (D1: +0x10 X)
#%0000 0100: 7ee000  01 00 28 00 0a 00 00 00 00 00 00 03 00 00 00 00 (D2: +0x20 X)
#%0000 1000: 7ee000  01 00 48 00 0a 00 00 00 00 00 00 03 00 00 00 00 (D3: +0x40 X)

#%1000 0000: 7ee000  01 00 08 00 0a 00 00 00 00 00 00 03 00 00 00 00 (D7: Does nothing? Should be +0x400 X)

C8/8FF8:	FF FF FFFF FFFFFF FF

C1: Forest Tileset

675000FF = Acid trip

Ti: Tileset		(Read at $c80400,x}
Examples:
-- 80 brings up tilesets 0 and 1 and animates the water wheels in Room 0E.
-- 40 brings up alternate sprites in place of animated sections, but is overriden by 80?
Cl: Palette		{Read at $cb0400,x?}
Effect
Ag
NP: What palette set to use for the NPCs. Since up to 9 of them can be loaded, there's
limited palette space for them, and they're rarely removed from the area (save for cutscenes).
Enemies only show up 3 at a time, so FF is often found where there are enemies and nothing else.

Fl: Event flag to check for whether or not to make an enemy/NPC appear.
Rg: Flag range to be checked.
X, Y: Shorthand coordinates for where the enemy/NPC will be in the area. Not sure what it's multiplied by.

Evnt: XX4X = Talk To
      XX1X = Auto-Talk
      XX8X = NPC Pushable

00: 8x8 Tileset
01: Map Palette Id
02: 16x16 Tileset
03: 04 =  OnEnter Event, 01 = SpecialTile Event
04: 80 = Use Flammie Drum, 20 = Use Magic Rope
05: Display Settings Index
06: ???
07: NPC Palette Id (FF = Battle Map)

 00 01 02 03 04 05 06 07

08E2E5: 9D82E1          STA $E182,X         ;Store 0x01 into [CURRENT_HIT_POINTS(Low), X].
08E2E8: 9E83E1          STZ $E183,X         ;Store Zero into [CURRENT_HIT_POINTS(High), X].
08E2EB: A580            LDA $80             ;Load {TempVar80} into Accumulator.
08E2ED: 0940            ORA #$40            ;Set 0x40.
08E2EF: 8580            STA $80             ;Store result back into {TempVar80}.
08E2F1: 22C05BC0        JSR $C05BC0         ;Jump to [Bank 00: FUNCTION_LOAD_STILL_ALIVE_MESSAGE_TO_MEMORY].
08E2F5: 60              RTS                 ;Return.

[FUNCTION_TRAP_GIVE_WEAPON_ORB 0x08E2F6-0x08E300]
C8/E2F6:	A905    	LDA #$05	;Load 0x05 into Accumulator.
C8/E2F8:	EB      	XBA 		;Switch A & B Accumulators.
C8/E2F9:	B9C9E1  	LDA $E1C9,Y	;Load [ENEMY_GP_DROP(High), Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E2FC:	22008001	JSR $018000
C8/E300:	60      	RTS		;Return.

[StatusEffect_MainHandler]
08E301: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E303: 6498            STZ $98         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E305: A59A            LDA $9A         ;Load {TempVar:SpellStatusEffects} into Accumulator.
08E307: 0598            ORA $98         ;Set {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects} to {TempVar:SpellStatusEffects}.
08E309: 8598            STA $98         ;Store result back into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E30B: BDAAE1          LDA $E1AA,X     ;Load [STATUS_IMMUNITY, X] into Accumulator.
08E30E: 49FFFF          EOR #$FFFF      ;Exclusive OR with [STATUS_IMMUNITY, X]. (This will get the status effects you can be inflicted with)
08E311: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:VulnerableStatusEffects}.
08E313: 2598            AND $98         ;AND with {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E315: 8598            STA $98         ;Store result back into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E317: 649A            STZ $9A         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:SpellStatusEffects}.
08E319: 1D90E1          ORA $E190,X     ;Set any active status effects.
08E31C: 25A4            AND $A4         ;Remove any that the character is immune to.
08E31E: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E321: BDEEE1          LDA $E1EE,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_SCRATCHPAD, X] into Accumulator.
08E324: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store [STATUS_EFFECTS_SCRATCHPAD, X] into {TempVar:StatusEffectScratchPad}.
08E326: 49FCFF          EOR #$FFFC      ;Remove 'Stun' and 'Injured'.
08E329: 3D90E1          AND $E190,X     ;Remove any status effect not present.
08E32C: 0598            ORA $98         ;Set any status effects that are present.
08E32E: 8598            STA $98         ;Store result back into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E330: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E333: 29FCFF          AND #$FFFC      ;Remove 'Stun' and 'Injured'.
08E336: DDEEE1          CMP $E1EE,X     ;Compare results with [STATUS_EFFECTS_SCRATCHPAD, X].
08E339: D007            BNE $E342       ;Branch to next section if they aren't equal. (Actor inflicted with a new status effect)
08E33B: A598            LDA $98         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects} into Accumulator.
08E33D: D00C            BNE $E34B       ;Branch over next section if it's non-zero.
08E33F: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E341: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

08E342: BDFAE1          LDA $E1FA,X     ;Load [7E/E1FA, X] into Accumulator.
08E345: 090200          ORA #$0002      ;Set 0x0002.
08E348: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store result back into [7E/E1FA, X].

08E34B: A9FFFF          LDA #$FFFF      ;Load 0xFFFF into Accumulator.
08E34E: 85B0            STA $B0         ;Store 0xFFFF into {TempVar:B0}.
08E350: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E353: 49FFFF          EOR #$FFFF      ;Exclusive OR with [STATUS_EFFECTS, X]. (Get status effects you aren't inflicted with)
08E355: 3DEEE1          AND $E1EE,X     ;AND that result with [STATUS_EFFECTS_SCRATCHPAD, X]. (Get only status effects that are in E1EE but not E190)
08E359: 85AE            STA $AE         ;Store the result into {TempVar:NewStatusEffects}.
08E35B: 1005            BPL $E362       ;Branch to next section if result is positive.
08E35D: 2041E4          JSR $E441       ;Jump to StatusEffectGhost_RemovalHandler.
08E360: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.

08E362: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E365: 100D            BPL $E374       ;Branch if character isn't dead.
08E367: 290080          AND #$8000      ;Remove all status effects except for 'Ghost'.
08E36A: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E36D: A598            LDA $98         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects} into Accumulator.
08E36F: 290080          AND #$8000      ;Remove all status effects except for 'Ghost'.
08E372: 8598            STA $98         ;Store result back into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.

08E374: A598            LDA $98         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects} into Accumulator.
08E376: 100A            BPL $E382       ;Branch if character isn't dead.
08E378: A90080          LDA #$8000      ;Load 0x8000 into Accumulator. (We're -REALLY- making sure the target is dead)
08E37B: 8598            STA $98         ;Store 0x8000 into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E37D: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store 0x8000 into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E380: 64B0            STZ $B0         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:B0}.

08E391: 200FE5          JSR $E50F       ;Jump to CompatibilityMonitor_StatusTimer05.

08E3B3: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08E3B4: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:MagicDefense} from {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E3B6: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E3B8: 1002            BPL $E3BC       ;Branch over next instruction if result is positive.
08E3BA: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.

08E3C0: A914            LDA #$14        ;Load 0x14 into Accumulator.

[StatusEffectGhost_RemovalHandler]
;     This will be called when a character (or boss) gets revived.
;     It has a bug in it. It is supposed to re-initialize NPC AI and re-run the character setup, however it never happens.
;     Instead of checking the controller values (located at [7E/00D9], [7E/00DA], and [7E/00DB]) its checking the hard values of 0xD9, 0xDA, 0xDB instead.
;     Additionally, the controller values are generally accessed via the DP Register, which currently holds the wrong value to access them.
;     DP Register is 0300 at this point.
08E441: A90000          LDA #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Accumulator.
08E444: 8598            STA $98         ;Store 0x0000 into {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects}.
08E446: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store 0x0000 into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E449: 85B0            STA $B0         ;Store 0x0000 into {TempVar:B0}.
08E44B: 9DF1E1          STA $E1F1,X     ;Store 0x0000 into [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTS, X].
08E44E: 9DF5E1          STA $E1F5,X     ;Store 0x0000 into [DAMAGE_MANA_POINTS, X] & [RESTORE_MANA_POINTS, X].
08E451: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E453: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E456: 3001            BMI $E459       ;Branch if it is a player character.
08E458: 60              RTS             ;Return.

08E459: A581            LDA $81         ;Load {TempVar:PlayerCharacterId} into Accumulator. (Either 00, 01, or 02)
08E45B: 1A              INC A           ;Increment the value.
08E45C: C903            CMP #$03        ;Compare the result against 0x03.
08E45E: D001            BNE $E461       ;Branch over the next instruction if the value isn't 0x03.
08E460: 1A              INC A           ;Increment the value to 0x04. (To get Popoie's offset)
08E461: 85AC            STA $AC         ;Store result into {TempVar:TempCharacterOffset}.
08E463: A9D9            LDA #$D9        ;Load 0xD9 into Accumulator.
08E465: 2907            AND #$07        ;Turn 0xD9 into 0x01. (Yeah...)
08E467: C5AC            CMP $AC         ;Compare {TempVar:TempCharacterOffset} against 0x01.
08E469: F01B            BEQ $E486       ;Branch out of routine if the values are equal.
08E46B: A9DA            LDA #$DA        ;Load 0xDA into Accumulator.
08E46D: 2907            AND #$07        ;Turn 0xD9 into 0x02. (Yeah...)
08E46F: C5AC            CMP $AC         ;Compare {TempVar:TempCharacterOffset} against 0x02.
08E471: F013            BEQ $E486       ;Branch out of routine if the values are equal.
08E473: A9DB            LDA #$DB        ;Load 0xDB into Accumulator.
08E475: 2907            AND #$07        ;Turn 0xD9 into 0x03. (Yeah...)
08E477: C5AC            CMP $AC         ;Compare {TempVar:TempCharacterOffset} against 0x03.
08E479: F00B            BEQ $E486       ;Branch out of routine if the values are equal.
08E47B: A581            LDA $81         ;Load {TempVar:PlayerCharacterId} into Accumulator.
08E47D: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E47E: 6980            ADC #$80        ;Add 0x80 to {TempVar:PlayerCharacterId}.
08E480: 222000C1        JSR $C10020     ;Re-initialize character AI and stats.
08E484: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset} into Register X.
08E486: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CompatibilityMonitor_StatusTimer01]
08E487: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E489: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E48C: 894000          BIT #$0040      ;Test for 'Petrify'.
08E48F: F00D            BEQ $E49E       ;If Actor doesn't have Petrify status then branch to CompatibilityMonitor_FrostyStatus.
08E491: 29C09A          AND #$9AC0      ;Remove 'Engulf', 'Poison', 'Barrel', 'Balloon', 'Frosty', 'Knocked Out', 'Paralyze', 'Tangle', 'Injured', and 'Stun'.
08E494: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E497: 5E82E1          LSR $E182,X     ;Divide [CURRENT_HIT_POINTS, X] by 2.
08E49A: FE82E1          INC $E182,X     ;Increment [CURRENT_HIT_POINTS, X].
08E49D: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CompatibilityMonitor_FrostyStatus]

08E4A3: 29E09A          AND #$9AE0      ;Remove 'Engulf', 'Poison', 'Barrel', 'Balloon', 'Knocked Out', 'Paralyze', 'Tangle', 'Injured', and 'Stun'.
08E4A6: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].

[CompatibilityMonitor_KnockedOut]

08E4AF: 29F0FB          AND #$FBF0      ;Remove 'Barrel', 'Paralyze', 'Tangle', 'Injured', and 'Stun'.
08E4B2: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E4B5: BDB1E1          LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
08E4B8: 090100          ORA #$0001      ;Set 'Knocked Out'.
08E4BB: 9DB1E1          STA $E1B1,X     ;Store it back into [MISC_FLAGS, X].
08E4BE: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CompatibilityMonitor_Paralyze]
;     This status effect is dummied and removes itself if ever inflicted.
;     It lowers your Agility by AGI/16, but is otherwise unfinished. You can still move, though your character will not animate.
08E4BF: 890800          BIT #$0008      ;Test for 'Paralyze'.
08E4C2: F007            BEQ $E4CB       ;If Actor doesn't have Paralyze status then branch to CompatibilityMonitor_Tangle.
08E4C4: 29F0FB          AND #$FBF0      ;Remove 'Barrel', 'Paralyze', 'Tangle', 'Injured', and 'Stun'. (The dummied status effect removes itself!)
08E4C7: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E4CA: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CompatibilityMonitor_Tangle]

08E4D3: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E4D6: 60              RTS             ;Return.

08E526: F006            BEQ $E52E       ;Branch out of routine none of them are set.
08E528: 29FFDF          AND #$DFFF      ;Remove 'Poison' status.
08E52B: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08E52E: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[StatusEffect_SpriteChangeHandler]
08E52F: 64A4            STZ $A4         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:NewSpriteId}.
08E531: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E534: 8902            BIT #$02        ;Test for 'Pygmy'.
08E536: F002            BEQ $E53A       ;Branch over next instruction if not set.
08E538: E6A4            INC $A4         ;Increment {TempVar:NewSpriteId}.
08E53A: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E53D: 3003            BMI $E542       ;Branch over next instruction if it's a Player Character.
08E53F: 4CA3E5          JMP $E5A3       ;Jump to StatusEffect_EnemySpriteChangeHandler.

08E542: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_01, X] into Accumulator.
08E545: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Test for 'Petrify'.
08E547: F007            BEQ $E550       ;Branch to next section if character isn't Petrify'd.
08E549: A9EC            LDA #$EC        ;Load 0xEC into Accumulator. (EC: Statue, ED: Pygmy Statue)
08E54B: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E54C: 65A4            ADC $A4         ;Add {TempVar:NewSpriteId} to 0xEC.
08E54E: 8042            BRA $E592       ;Branch down to testing the sprite Id.

08E550: 8920            BIT #$20        ;Test for 'Frosty'.
08E552: F007            BEQ $E55B       ;Branch to next section if character is not Frosty'd.
08E554: A9EE            LDA #$EE        ;Load 0xEE into Accumulator.
08E556: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E557: 65A4            ADC $A4         ;Add {TempVar:NewSpriteId} to 0xEE. (EE: Snowman, ED: Pygmy Snowman)
08E559: 8037            BRA $E592       ;Branch down to testing the sprite Id.

08E55B: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E55E: 1008            BPL $E568       ;Branch to next section if character isn't dead.
08E560: A581            LDA $81         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterId} into Accumulator.
08E562: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E563: 69E4            ADC #$E4        ;Add 0xE4 to {TempVar:CharacterId}. (E4, E5, E6: Ghost Sprites)
08E565: 4C92E5          JMP $E592       ;Jump down to testing the sprite Id.

08E568: 8910            BIT #$10        ;Test for 'Moogle'.
08E56A: F017            BEQ $E583       ;Branch down two sections if character isn't Moogle'd.
08E56C: BDE7E1          LDA $E1E7,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_ID, X] into Accumulator.
08E56F: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E570: 6970            ADC #$70        ;Add 0x70 to [CHARACTER_ID, X]. (80, 81, 82 + 70 = Characters Moogle sprite)
08E572: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store result into {TempVar:MoogleSpriteId}.
08E574: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:NewSpriteId} into Accumulator.
08E576: F007            BEQ $E57F       ;Branch to next section if {TempVar:NewSpriteId} is zero. (Character isn't Pygmy'd)
08E578: A5A6            LDA $A6         ;Load {TempVar:MoogleSpriteId} into Accumulator.
08E57A: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E57B: 6903            ADC #$03        ;Add 0x03 to {TempVar:MoogleSpriteId}. (To get Id for Pygmy Moogle Sprite)
08E57D: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store result into {TempVar:NewMoogleSpriteId}.

08E57F: A5A6            LDA $A6         ;Load {TempVar:MoogleSpriteId} into Accumulator.
08E581: 800F            BRA $E592       ;Branch down to testing the sprite Id.

08E583: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:NewSpriteId} into Accumulator.
08E585: F008            BEQ $E58F       ;Branch to next section if {TempVar:NewSpriteId} is zero.
08E587: BDE7E1          LDA $E1E7,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_ID, X] into Accumulator.
08E58A: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E58B: 6976            ADC #$76        ;Add 0x76 to [CHARACTER_ID, X]. (To get normal Moogle Sprite)
08E58D: 8003            BRA $E592       ;Branch over the next instruction.
08E58F: BDE7E1          LDA $E1E7,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_ID, X] into Accumulator.
 
08E592: DD80E1          CMP $E180,X     ;Compare value against [SPRITE_ID, X]. (Value is either one of the Statue/Snowman Ids, [CHARACTER_ID, X] or [CHARACTER_ID, X] + 0x76)
08E595: F00B            BEQ $E5A2       ;Branch out of the routine if they are equal.
08E597: 9D80E1          STA $E180,X     ;Store the new sprite Id back into [CHARACTER_ID, X].
08E59A: BDFAE1          LDA $E1FA,X     ;Load [7E/E1FA, X] into Accumulator.
08E59D: 0901            ORA #$01        ;Set 0x01.
08E59F: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store result back into [7E/E1FA, X].
08E5A2: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[StatusEffect_EnemySpriteChangeHandler]
08E5A3: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E5A6: 1001            BPL $E5A9       ;Branch over next instruction if the enemy isn't dead.
08E5A8: 60              RTS             ;Return.

08E5A9: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_01,X] into Accumulator.
08E5AC: 8920            BIT #$20        ;Test for 'Frosty'.

08E5B0: A983            LDA #$83        ;Load 0x83 into Accumulator. (83: Monster Snowman)
08E5B2: 80DE            BRA $E592       ;Branch up to testing sprite Id.

08E5B4: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E5B7: 8908            BIT #$08        ;Test for 'Transform'.
08E5B9: F0D4            BEQ $E58F       ;Branch up to testing the sprite Id if 'Transform' is not set.

08E5BB: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E5BD: 229C38C0        JSR $C0389C     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberLong.
08E5C1: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E5C3: 290700          AND #$0007      ;Get a number between 0x00 and 0x07. (Calling GetRangeNumberRange instead was to hard?)
08E5C6: AA              TAX             ;Transfer Accumulator to Register X.
08E5C7: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E5C9: BFF3E5C8        LDA $C8E5F3,X   ;Load a value from Table_TransformTypes.
08E5CD: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset} into Register X.
08E5CF: 9D80E1          STA $E180,X     ;Store result into [SPRITE_ID, X].
08E5D2: BDFAE1          LDA $E1FA,X     ;Load [7E/E1FA, X] into Accumulator.
08E5D5: 0907            ORA #$07        ;Set 0x07.
08E5D7: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store result back into [7E/E1FA, X].
08E5DA: 223045C0        JSR $C04530     ;Jump to MasterCharacterSetup.
08E5DE: BDE8E1          LDA $E1E8,X     ;Load [WEAPON_01, X] into Accumulator.

08E5E3: BDE4E1          LDA $E1E4,X     ;Load [WEAPON_TYPE_ID, X] into Accumulator.
08E5E6: 1005            BPL $E5ED       ;Branch to end of routine if positive.
08E5E8: BDE9E1          LDA $E1E9,X     ;Load [WEAPON_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E5EB: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store [WEAPON_02, X] into {TempVar:Weapon02}.

[Table_TransformTypes]
08E5F3: 00                              [00: Rabite]
08E5F4: 02                              [01: Mushboom]

[StatusEffect_WeaponChangeHandlerLong]
;     Used by the Mana Magic handlers in Bank 00 to force equip the Mana Sword.
08E5FB: 20FFE5          JSR $E5FF       ;Jump to StatusEffect_WeaponChangeHandler.
08E5FE: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

[StatusEffect_WeaponChangeHandler]
;     A couple things of interest here.
;     Enemies have a Barrel weapon. While not inherit, every enemy has immunity set to this status effect.
;     Also interesting: An Enemy with Ghost, Petrify, Frosty, or Moogle gets equipped with a Spike Knuckle.
;     
;     A weapon with Balloon status effect will (almost) always cause the low level Balloon to appear.
;     There is an exception. Unlike the SpellPower temporary variable, the elemental variable is not reset every frame (or ever).
;     Thus a weapon with Balloon will have the elemental level of the last spell cast.
;          
;     The Grim Reaper weapon stays equipped for 150 frames.
;     Other status effect weapons stay equipped for as long as the effect lasts.
;
;     Level 00-03 Balloon spell causes the Low Level Balloon.
;     Level 04-07 Balloon spell causes the Mid Level Balloon.
;     Level 08 Balloon spell causes the Moogle Balloon.
08E5FF: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E602: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Test for 'Boss'.
08E604: D0EC            BNE $E5F2       ;Branch out of routine if character is a Boss.

08E606: 64A4            STZ $A4         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.
08E608: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E60B: 1024            BPL $E631       ;Branch down to Pygmy testing if character is not dead.

08E60D: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E610: 1016            BPL $E628       ;Branch to Shadow Clone testing if not a player character.

08E612: A599            LDA $99         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects (High)} into Accumulator.
08E614: F00D            BEQ $E623       ;If {TempVar:CurrentStatusEffects (High)} is zero then branch over next section.
08E616: A91E            LDA #$1E        ;Load 0x1E into Accumulator.
08E618: 9DB9E1          STA $E1B9,X     ;Store 0x1E into [MAIN_TIMER, X].
08E61B: 9E75E1          STZ $E175,X     ;Store Zero into [SPLASH_NUMBERS_ACTIVATOR, X].
08E61E: A9FF            LDA #$FF        ;Load 0xFF into Accumulator. (Grim Reaper Weapon)
08E620: 4CC3E6          JMP $E6C3       ;Jump and equip new weapon.

08E623: A9F1            LDA #$F1        ;Load 0xF1 into Accumulator. (F1: Frosty, Petrify, Moogle Weapon)
08E625: 4CC3E6          JMP $E6C3       ;Jump and equip new weapon.

08E628: 8901            BIT #$01        ;Test for 'Shadow Clone'.
08E62A: D023            BNE $E64F       ;Branch out of routine if it's a Shadow Clone.
08E62C: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator. (So dead enemies equip a Spike Knuckle)
08E62E: 4CC3E6          JMP $E6C3       ;Jump and equip new weapon.

08E631: 8902            BIT #$02        ;Test for 'Pygmy'.
08E633: F002            BEQ $E637       ;Branch to next section if 'Pygmy' is not set.
08E635: E6A4            INC $A4         ;Increment {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.

08E637: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Test for 'Engulf'.
08E639: F015            BEQ $E650       ;Branch to StatusEffect_BalloonWeaponChangeHandler if 'Engulf' is not set.
08E63B: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E63E: 8981            BIT #$81        ;Test for 'Player Character' & 'Shadow Clone'.
08E640: D005            BNE $E647       ;Branch to next section if either of those are set.
08E642: A9F6            LDA #$F6        ;Load 0xF6 into Accumulator. (Enemy Engulf Weapon)
08E644: 4CC3E6          JMP $E6C3       ;Jump and equip new weapon.

08E647: A9FD            LDA #$FD        ;Load 0xFD into Accumulator. (FD: Engulf Weapon, FE: Pygmy Engulf Weapon).
08E649: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E64A: 65A4            ADC $A4         ;Add {TempVar:PygmyOffset} to 0xFD.
08E64C: 4CC3E6          JMP $E6C3       ;Jump and equip new weapon.
08E64F: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[StatusEffect_BalloonWeaponChangeHandler]
08E650: 8901            BIT #$01        ;Test for 'Balloon'.
08E652: F026            BEQ $E67A       ;Branch to StatusEffect_GeneralWeaponChangeHandler if 'Balloon' is not set.
08E654: A9F7            LDA #$F7        ;Load 0xF7 into Accumulator. (F7: Low Balloon Weapon, F8: Mid Balloon Weapon, F9: Moogle Balloon Weapon)
08E656: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store 0xF7 into {TempVar:WeaponId}.
08E658: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E65B: 8981            BIT #$81        ;Test for 'Player Character' & 'Shadow Clone'.
08E65D: D004            BNE $E663       ;Branch to next section if either of those are set.
08E65F: A9F2            LDA #$F2        ;Load 0xF2 into Accumulator. (F2: Enemy Low Balloon Weapon, F3: Enemy Mid Balloon Weapon, F4: Enemy Moogle Balloon Weapon)
08E661: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store 0xF2 into {TempVar:WeaponId}.

08E663: 64A4            STZ $A4         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.
08E665: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel} into Accumulator.
08E667: C904            CMP #$04        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} against 0x04.
08E669: 9008            BCC $E673       ;Branch to next section if {TempElementalLevel} is less than 0x04.
08E66B: E6A4            INC $A4         ;Increment {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.
08E66D: C908            CMP #$08        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} against 0x08.
08E66F: 9002            BCC $E673       ;Branch to next section if {TempElementalLevel} is less than 0x08.
08E671: E6A4            INC $A4         ;Increment {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.

08E673: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:PygmyOffset} into Accumulator.
08E675: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E676: 65A6            ADC $A6         ;Add {TempVar:WeaponId} to {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.
08E678: 8049            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip new weapon.

[StatusEffect_GeneralWeaponChangeHandler]
08E67A: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E67C: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E67F: 896010          BIT #$1060      ;Test for 'Moogle', 'Petrify', and 'Frosty'.
08E682: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E684: F00F            BEQ $E695       ;Branch if none of those are set.
08E686: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E689: 8981            BIT #$81        ;Test for 'Player Character' and 'Shadow Clone'.
08E68B: D004            BNE $E691       ;Branch over next section if either of those are set.

08E68D: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator. (So an enemy with Moogle, Petrify or Frosty gets equipped with a Spike Knuckle)
08E68F: 8032            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip new weapon.
 
08E691: A9F1            LDA #$F1        ;Load 0xF1 into Accumulator. (F1: Frosty, Petrify, Moogle Weapon)
08E693: 802E            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip new weapon.

08E695: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E698: 8904            BIT #$04        ;Test for 'Barrel'.
08E69A: F012            BEQ $E6AE       ;Branch if either of those are set.
08E69C: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E69F: 8981            BIT #$81        ;Test for 'Player Character' and 'Shadow Clone'.
08E6A1: D004            BNE $E6A7       ;Branch over next section if either of those are set.

08E6A3: A9F5            LDA #$F5        ;Load 0xF5 into Accumulator. (F5: Enemy Barrel Weapon)
08E6A5: 801C            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip the new weapon.

08E6A7: A9FB            LDA #$FB        ;Load 0xFB into Accumulator.  (FB: Barrel Weapon, FC: Pygmy Barrel Weapon)
08E6A9: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E6AA: 65A4            ADC $A4         ;Add 0xFB to {TempVar:PygmyOffset}.
08E6AC: 8015            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip the new weapon.

08E6AE: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:PygmyOffset} into Accumulator.
08E6B0: F004            BEQ $E6B6       ;Branch to next section if it's zero.
08E6B2: A9FA            LDA #$FA        ;Load 0xFA into Accumulator. (FA: Pygmy Kick Weapon)
08E6B4: 800D            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip the new weapon.

08E6B6: BDE4E1          LDA $E1E4,X     ;Load [WEAPON_TYPE_ID, X] into Accumulator.
08E6B9: 1005            BPL $E6C0       ;Branch to next section if it isn't a status effect weapon.
08E6BB: BDE9E1          LDA $E1E9,X     ;Load [WEAPON_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E6BE: 8003            BRA $E6C3       ;Branch and equip the weapon.

08E6C0: BDE8E1          LDA $E1E8,X     ;Load [WEAPON_01, X] into Accumulator. (This will undo the weapon change)
08E6C3: DDE3E1          CMP $E1E3,X     ;Compare against [EQUIPPED_WEAPON_ID, X].
08E6C6: F00B            BEQ $E6D3       ;Branch out of routine if equal.
08E6C8: 9DE3E1          STA $E1E3,X     ;Otherwise store the new weapon Id into [EQUIPPED_WEAPON_ID, X].
08E6CB: A904            LDA #$04        ;Load 0x04 into Accumulator.
08E6CD: 1DFAE1          ORA $E1FA,X     ;Set 0x04 to [7E\E1FA, X].
08E6D0: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store result back into [7E\E1FA, X].
08E6D3: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CastSpellLong]
;     X = Target
;     Y = Caster
C8/E6D4:	225E39C0	JSR $C0395E     ;Jump to CleanseTargetingOffsetsLong.
C8/E6D8:	A910    	LDA #$10        ;Load 0x10 into Accumulator.
C8/E6DA:	9DF8E1  	STA $E1F8,X     ;Store 0x10 into [7E:E1F8, X].
C8/E6DD:	99F8E1  	STA $E1F8,Y     ;Store 0x10 into [7E:E1F8, Y].
C8/E6E0:	B970E1  	LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E6E3:	8F024200	STA $004202     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into [WRMPYA].
C8/E6E7:	A940    	LDA #$40        ;Load 0x40 into Accumulator.
C8/E6E9:	8F034200	STA $004203     ;Store 0x40 into [WRMPYB].
C8/E6ED:	EA      	NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
C8/E6EE:	EA      	NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
C8/E6EF:	EA      	NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
C8/E6F0:	C220    	REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C8/E6F2:	AF164200	LDA $004216     ;Load [RDMPY] into Accumulator.
C8/E6F6:	85DC    	STA $DC         ;Store [RDMPY] into {TempVar:SpellInfomationAddress}.
C8/E6F8:	E220    	SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C8/E6FA:	A9D0    	LDA #$D0        ;Load 0xD0 into Accumulator. (Bank byte for spell data)
C8/E6FC:	85DE    	STA $DE         ;Store 0xD0 into {TempVar:SpellInfomationAddressBank}.
C8/E6FE:	B972E1  	LDA $E172,Y     ;Load [PRIMARY_SPELL_TARGET, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E701:	1024    	BPL $E727       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_TargetSearch if the slot is in use.
C8/E703:	B973E1  	LDA $E173,Y     ;Load [SECONDARY_SPELL_TARGET, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E706:	101F    	BPL $E727       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_TargetSearch if the slot is in use.
C8/E708:	B974E1  	LDA $E174,Y     ;Load [TERTIARY_SPELL_TARGET, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E70B:	101A    	BPL $E727       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_TargetSearch if the slot is in use.
C8/E70D:	22CD4DD0	JSR $D04DCD     ;Jump to TryCalculateSpellExperienceLong.
C8/E711:	A0DF2A    	LDY #$2ADF      ;Load 0x2ADF into Accumulator. (Offset to the spells mana cost)
C8/E714:	B7DC    	LDA [$DC],Y     ;Load the spells Mana Cost from DataTable_SpellInformation.
C8/E716:	A483    	LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
C8/E718:	85A4    	STA $A4         ;Store the spells Mana Cost into {TempVar:CastSpellManaCost}.
C8/E71A:	B986E1  	LDA $E186,Y     ;Load [CURRENT_MANA_POINTS, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E71D:	38      	SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
C8/E71E:	E5A4    	SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:CastSpellManaCost} from [CURRENT_MANA_POINTS, Y].
C8/E720:	1002    	BPL $E724       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_UpdateCasterMana if the caster still has MP left.
C8/E722:	A900    	LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.

[CastSpellLong_UpdateCasterMana]
C8/E724:	9986E1  	STA $E186,Y     ;Store remaining mana back into [CURRENT_MANA_POINTS, Y].

[CastSpellLong_TargetSearch]
C8/E727:	6491    	STZ $91         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:CasterStat}.

C8/E72B:	649B    	STZ $9B         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:SpellStatusEffectsH}.

[CastSpellLong_TargetLoopStart]
C8/E738:	DD72E1    	CMP $E172, X    ;Compare {TempVar:SpellTargetIndex} against [SPELL_TARGET, X].
C8/E73C:	F006    	BEQ $E744       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_Exit if the target has already been targeted.
C8/E73E:	E8      	INX             ;Increment Register X.
C8/E73F:	88      	DEY             ;Decrement Register Y.
C8/E740:	D0F7    	BNE $E738       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_TargetLoopStart if there are still target slots to check.	
C8/E742:	8001 +    	BRA $E745       ;Branch to CastSpellOnTargetLong.

[CastSpellLong_Exit]
C8/E744:	6B      	RTL             ;Return Long.

[CastSpellOnTargetLong]
C8/E745:	A683    	LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
C8/E747:	A00300		LDY #$0003      ;Load 0x0003 into Register Y.

[CastSpellOnTargetLong_LoopStart]
C8/E74A: -	BD72E1    	LDA $E172,X     ;Load [SPELL_TARGET, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E74D:	3006 +   	BMI $E755       ;Branch to CastSpellOnTargetLong_ValidateSpellCast if the current targetting index is available.
C8/E74F:	E8      	INX             ;Increment Register X.
C8/E750:	88      	DEY             ;Decrement Register Y. (But why tho? Not used for the loop and gets clobbered on exit)
C8/E751:	D0F7 -   	BNE $E74A       ;Branch to CastSpellOnTargetLong_LoopStart if here are still targeting indexes to check.
C8/E753:	80EF    	BRA $E744       ;Branch to CastSpellLong_Exit.

[CastSpellOnTargetLong_ValidateSpellCast]
C8/E755: +	A581    	LDA $81         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTargetIndex} into Accumulator.
C8/E757:	9D72E1  	STA $E172,X     ;Store {TempVar:SpellTargetIndex} into [SPELL_TARGET, X].
C8/E75A:	A685    	LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:TargetOffset} into Register X.
C8/E75C:	A483    	LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
C8/E75E:	B991E1  	LDA $E191,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E761:	301A +   	BMI $E77D       ;Branch to CastSpellOnTargetLong_Exit if the caster is dead.
C8/E763:	BDFBE1  	LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E766:	8910    	BIT #$10        ;Test for 'Crystal Orb'.
C8/E768:	D014 ++   	BNE $E77E       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong if the target is a not Crystal Orb.
C8/E76A:	B970E1  	LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E76D:	C921    	CMP #$21        ;Compare [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] against 0x21. (Revivifer)
C8/E76F:	F00D ++   	BEQ $E77E       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong if the caster is casting Revivifer. (Bypasses the target dead check that comes later)
C8/E771:	BD80E1  	LDA $E180,X     ;Load [SPRITE_ID, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E774:	C9EA    	CMP #$EA        ;Compare [SPRITE_ID, X] against 0xEA. (Treasure Chest)
C8/E776:	F006 ++   	BEQ $E77E       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong if the target is a Treasure Chest.
C8/E778:	BD91E1  	LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E77B:	1001 ++   	BPL $E77E       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong if the target isn't dead.

[CastSpellOnTargetLong_Exit]
C8/E77D: +	6B      	RTL             ;Return Long.
 
[PreCastSpellLong]
C8/E77E: ++	A582    	LDA $82         ;Load {TempVar:CasterID} into Accumulator.
C8/E780:	9DECE1  	STA $E1EC,X     ;Store {TempVar:CasterID} into [LAST_ATTACKER_ID, X].
C8/E783:	A9FF    	LDA #$FF        ;Load 0xFF into Accumulator.
C8/E785:	9DF0E1  	STA $E1F0,X     ;Store 0xFF into [ATTACKER_ID, X].
C8/E788:	A900    	LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
C8/E78A:	EB      	XBA             ;Switch A & B Accumulators.
C8/E78B:	BDFBE1  	LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E78E:	8910    	BIT #$10        ;Test for 'Crystal Orb'.
C8/E790:	F015    	BEQ $E7A7       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong if target is not a Crystal Orb. (So none of the spells effects happen to Crystal Orbs)
C8/E792:	B92CE0  	LDA $E02C,Y     ;Load [CURRENT_CONTROLLER, Y] into Accumulator.
C8/E795:	F00F    	BEQ $E7A6       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong_Exit if caster is AI controlled.
C8/E797:	3A      	DEC A           ;Decrement the current controller value.
C8/E798:	C903    	CMP #$03        ;Compare (controller value - 1) against 0x03.
C8/E79A:	D001    	BNE $E79D       ;Branch to PreCastSpellLong_DisableController if result isn't 0x03.
C8/E79C:	3A      	DEC A           ;Decrement the current controller value.

[PreCastSpellLong_DisableController]
C8/E79D:	AA      	TAX             ;Transfer the index to Register X.
C8/E79E:	BDD900  	LDA $00D9,X     ;Load [PLAYER_CONTROLLED_CHARACTER, X] into Accumulator.
C8/E7A1:	0980    	ORA #$80        ;Set 0x80. (Makes the game ignore Controller input).
C8/E7A3:	9DD900  	STA $00D9,X     ;Store result back into [PLAYER_CONTROLLED_CHARACTER, X].

[PreCastSpellLong_Exit]
C8/E7A6:	6B      	RTL             ;Return Long.
 
[StartCastSpellLong]
;     X = Target
;     Y = Caster
;     Well, this seems to be the reason for Spell-Cancelling.
;     If the target is a player then clear damage points.
;     If the caster is a player then clear damage points.
;     Also note worthy is this means that monsters can't spell cancel either.
08E7A7: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E7AA: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Test to see if it's a Boss.
08E7AC: F006            BEQ $E7B4       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong_ClearTargetDamage if the target is not a boss.
08E7AE: B970E1          LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
08E7B1: 9D0AE0          STA $E00A,X     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into [LAST_CAST_SPELL_ID, X].

[StartCastSpellLong_ClearTargetDamage]
08E7B4: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
08E7B7: 1006            BPL $E7BF       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong_ClearCasterDamage if the target is not a player.
08E7B9: 9EF1E1          STZ $E1F1,X     ;Store zero in [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTSL, X].
08E7BC: 9EF2E1          STZ $E1F2,X     ;Store zero in [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTSH, X].

[StartCastSpellLong_ClearCasterDamage]
08E7BF: B9FBE1          LDA $E1FB,Y     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, Y] into Accumulator.
08E7C2: 1008            BPL $E7CC       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong_GetSpellPower if the caster is not a player.
08E7C4: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08E7C6: 99F1E1          STA $E1F1,Y     ;Store 0x00 in [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTSL, Y].
08E7C9: 99F2E1          STA $E1F2,Y     ;Store 0x00 in [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTSH, Y].

[StartCastSpellLong_GetSpellPower]
08E7CC: 2055E8          JSR $E855       ;Jump to DetermineAndLoadPrimaryCastingStat.
08E7CF: B9FBE1          LDA $E1FB,Y     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, Y] into Accumulator.
08E7D2: 1009            BPL $E7DD       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong_StartCast if the caster is not a player.
08E7D4: A0DA2A          LDY #$2ADA      ;Load the offset to the spell power definition into Register Y.
08E7D7: B7DC            LDA [$DC],Y     ;Load the cast spells Power into Accumulator.
08E7D9: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
08E7DB: 8596            STA $96         ;Store cast spells Power in {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.

[StartCastSpellLong_StartCast]
08E7DD: B976E1          LDA $E176,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into Accumulator.
08E7E0: F01E            BEQ $E800       ;Branch to StartCastSpellLong_Exit if the spell has no targets.
08E7E2: BDB0E1          LDA $E1B0,X     ;Load [BUFF_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08E7E5: 48              PHA             ;Push [BUFF_EFFECTS, X] onto the stack.
08E7E6: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08E7E8: EB              XBA             ;Switch A & B Accumulators.
08E7E9: B970E1          LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
08E7EC: 0A              ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
08E7ED: AA              TAX             ;Transfer the pointer to Register X.
08E7EE: FC01E8          JSR ($E801,X)   ;Jump to PointerTable_SpellDefinitions, X.
08E7F1: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E7F3: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:TargetOffset} into Register X.
08E7F5: 68              PLA             ;Pull [BUFF_EFFECTS, X] off the stack.
08E7F6: 49FF            EOR #$FF        ;Exclusive OR with [BUFF_EFFECTS, X].
08E7F8: 3DB0E1          AND $E1B0,X     ;Logical AND with [BUFF_EFFECTS, X]. (Get only new buff effects)
08E7FB: A8              TAY             ;Transfer buff effects to Register Y.
08E7FC: 222159C0        JSR $C05921     ;Jump to PumpBuffAndDebuffMessagesLong.

[StartCastSpellLong_Exit]
08E800: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

[DetermineAndLoadPrimaryCastingStat]
;     If SpellId % 6 < 3, Spell uses INT. Otherwise it uses WIS.
;     Enemies use spell power defined in the enemy table instead of the spells defined power.
08E855: B970E1	        LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
08E858: 8F044200        STA $004204     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into [Dividend (Low)].
08E85C: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08E85E: 8F054200        STA $004205     ;Store it in [Dividend (High)].
08E862: A906            LDA #$06        ;Load 0x06 into Accumulator.
08E864: 8F064200        STA $004206     ;Store 0x06 into [Divisor B].
08E868: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E869: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86A: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86B: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86C: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86D: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86E: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E86F: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E870: AF164200        LDA $004216     ;Load [Remainder (Low)] into Accumulator.
08E874: C903            CMP #$03        ;Compare [Remainder (Low)] with 0x03.
08E876: 900B            BCC $E883       ;If it's less then branch and load Intelligence as a caster stat.
08E878: B9FEE1          LDA $E1FE,Y     ;Load [ENEMY_WHITE_MAGIC_POWER, Y] into Accumulator.
08E87B: 8596            STA $96         ;Store [ENEMY_WHITE_MAGIC_POWER, Y] into {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08E87D: B98CE1          LDA $E18C,Y     ;Load [WISDOM, Y] into Accumulator.
08E880: 8591            STA $91         ;Store [WISDOM, Y] into [TempVar:CasterStat].
08E882: 60              RTS             ;Return.

08E883: B98BE1          LDA $E18B,Y     ;Load [INTELLIGENCE, Y] into Accumulator.
08E886: 8591            STA $91         ;Store [INTELLIGENCE, Y] into [TempVar:CasterStat].
08E888: B9FDE1          LDA $E1FD,Y     ;Load [ENEMY_BLACK_MAGIC_POWER, Y] into Accumulator.
08E88B: 8596            STA $96         ;Store [ENEMY_BLACK_MAGIC_POWER, Y] into {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08E88D: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LoadAndInflictSpellStatusEffects]

08E890: A0DD2A          LDY #$2ADD      ;Load [SpellDefinitionStatusEffectPointer] into Register Y.

08E89B: 2201E3C8        JSR $C8E301     ;Jump to StatusEffect_MainHandler
08E89F: 60              RTS             ;Return

[SetElementalResistanceValue]
08E8A0: A0DC2A          LDY #$2ADC      ;Load [SpellDefinitionElementPointer] into Register Y.
08E8A3: B7DC            LDA [$DC],Y     ;Load [CastSpellElement] into Accumulator.
08E8A5: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:SpellCasterOffset} into Register Y.
08E8A7: 3CA3E1          BIT $E1A3,X     ;Test [CastSpellElement] vs [STRONG_ELEMENT, X] to see if target is strong against this element.
08E8AA: F005            BEQ $E8B1       ;If not branch and test for weakness.
08E8AC: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08E8AE: 858F            STA $8F         ;Store 0x01 into {TempVar:WeaknessVar}. (Target is strong against this spell element)
08E8B0: 60              RTS             ;Return.
 
08E8B1: 3CA1E1          BIT $E1A1,X     ;Test [CastSpellElement] vs [WEAK_ELEMENT, X] to see if target is weak against this element.
08E8B4: F005            BEQ $E8BB       ;If not branch and set the target as neutral to this element.
08E8B6: A904            LDA #$04        ;Load 0x04 into Accumulator.
08E8B8: 858F            STA $8F         ;Store 0x04 into {TempVar:WeaknessVar}. (Target is weak against this spell element)
08E8BA: 60              RTS             ;Return.
 
08E8BB: A902            LDA #$02        ;Load 0x02 into Accumulator.
08E8BD: 858F            STA $8F         ;Store 0x02 into {TempVar:WeaknessVar}. (Target is neutral against this spell element)
08E8BF: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CalculateSpellPowerAndTimer]
;     Spell Accuracy: (PrimaryCasterStat / 4) + Base Spell Accuracy
;     Base Spell Power: (PrimaryCasterStat + Base Spell Power) * (ElementalLevel + 2)
;     If [Rnd(0..101)] < [Spell Accuracy] Then
;         TempVar: Base Spell Power + ManaPower2
;         Calculated Spell Power: TempVar + [Rnd(0..TempVar/16)]
;     Else
;         Calculated Spell Power: (SACC * [BSP + {BSP / 4}]) / 100
;     
;     If Target = WeakToElement Then
;         Calculated Spell Power: Calculated Spell Power * 2
;     Else If Target = StrongToElement Then
;         Calculated Spell Power: Calculated Spell Power / 2
;     
;     Total Spell Power: Calculated Spell Power / NumberOfTargets
08E8C0: 2090E9          JSR $E990       ;Jump to CalculateMagicDefense.
08E8C3: A591            LDA $91         ;Load {TempVar:CasterStat}.
08E8C5: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E8C6: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E8C7: 858D            STA $8D         ;Store in {TempVar:SpellAccuracy}.
08E8C9: A0DB2A          LDY #$2ADB      ;Load [SpellDefinitionAccuracyPointer] into Register Y.
08E8CC: B7DC            LDA [$DC],Y     ;Load [CastSpellAccuracy]. (Always 4B).
08E8CE: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
08E8D0: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E8D1: 658D            ADC $8D         ;Add {TempVar:SpellAccuracy} to [CastSpellAccuracy].
08E8D3: C963            CMP #$63        ;Compare with 0x63.(99)
08E8D5: 9002            BCC $E8D9       ;If it's less then branch over next instruction.
08E8D7: A963            LDA #$63        ;Load 0x63 into Accumulator.
08E8D9: 858D            STA $8D         ;Store into {TempVar:SpellAccuracy}.
08E8DB: A596            LDA $96         ;Load {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08E8DD: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E8DE: 6591            ADC $91         ;Add {TempVar:CastSpellPower} to {TempVar:CasterStat}.
08E8E0: 85D4            STA $D4         ;Store in {TempVar:TotalSpellPower (Low)}.
 
08E8E2: 64D5            STZ $D5         ;Store Zero in {TempVar:TotalSpellPower(High)}.
08E8E4: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel}.
08E8E6: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E8E7: 6902            ADC #$02        ;Add 0x02 to {TempVar:ElementalLevel}.
08E8E9: 85D0            STA $D0         ;Store in {TempVar:BoostedElementalLevel}.
08E8EB: 22B254C0        JSR $C054B2     ;Jump to MultiplyPowerByLevelLong.
 
08E8EF: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E8F1: A5CC            LDA $CC         ;Load {TempVar:CalculatedResult} into Accumulator.
08E8F3: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide it by 2.
08E8F4: C9E703          CMP #$03E7      ;Compare {TempVar:CalculatedResult} against 0x03E7(999).
08E8F7: 9003            BCC $E8FC       ;Branch over next instruction if it is less than that.
08E8F9: A9E703          LDA #$03E7      ;Load 0x03E7 into Accumulator.
08E8FC: 8589            STA $89         ;Store into {TempVar:SpellDamage}.
08E8FE: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E900: A965            LDA #$65        ;Load 0x65 into Accumulator.
08E902: 228438C0        JSR $C03884     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberRangeLong.

08E906: C58D            CMP $8D         ;Compare [Rnd(0..100)] to {TempVar:SpellAccuracy}.
08E908: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E90A: B031            BCS $E93D       ;Branch ahead if it's greater. (Spell missed)
 
08E90C: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E90E: B9E6E1          LDA $E1E6,Y     ;Load [MANA_POWER, Y] into Accumulator.
08E911: 8F024200        STA $004202     ;Store [MANA_POWER, Y] into [Multiplicand A].
08E915: 8F034200        STA $004203     ;Store [MANA_POWER, Y] into [Multplier B].
08E919: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E91A: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E91B: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E91C: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E91E: AF164200        LDA $004216     ;Load [Product] into Accumulator.
08E922: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E923: 6589            ADC $89         ;Add [Product(MANA_POWER ^ 2)] to {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E925: 8589            STA $89         ;Store result back into {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E927: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E928: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.(04)
08E929: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.(08)
08E92A: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.(16)
08E92B: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E92D: 228438C0        JSR $C03884     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberRangeLong.
08E931: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E933: 29FF00          AND #$00FF      ;Drop off the high byte. (Now we have our spell variance)
08E936: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E937: 6589            ADC $89         ;Add the variance to {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E939: 8589            STA $89         ;Store into {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E93B: 8014            BRA $E951       ;Branch over failure calculation.
 
08E93D: A589            LDA $89         ;Load {TempVar:SpellPower} into Accumulator.
08E93F: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store it in {TempVar:MultiplicandB}.
08E941: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E943: A58D            LDA $8D         ;Load {TempVar:SpellAccuracy} into Accumulator.
08E945: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store it in {TempVar:MultiplicandA}.
08E947: 223754C0        JSR $C05437     ;Jump to CalculateFailureValueLong.
08E94B: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E94D: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:Result} into Accumulator.
08E94F: 8589            STA $89         ;Store it in {TempVar:SpellDamage}.

08E951: A58F            LDA $8F         ;Load {TempVar:WeaknessVar} into Accumulator. 0x01 = Strong. 0x02 = Normal. 0x04 = Weak.
08E953: 29FF00          AND #$00FF      ;Drop off the high byte.
08E956: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store that into {TempVar:SpellDamageLoopCounter}.
08E958: A90000          LDA #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Accumulator.
08E95B: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E95C: 6589            ADC $89         ;Add Accumulator to {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E95E: C6A4            DEC $A4         ;Decrement {TempVar:SpellDamageLoopCounter}.
08E960: D0F9            BNE $E95B       ;If its not zero then branch back.
08E962: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E963: 8F044200        STA $004204     ;Store Accumulator into [Dividend].
08E967: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E969: B976E1          LDA $E176,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into Accumulator.
08E96C: 8F064200        STA $004206     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into [Divisor B].
08E970: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E971: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E972: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E973: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E974: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E975: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E976: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (Waiting on Results)
08E977: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E979: AF144200        LDA $004214     ;Load [Quotient] into Accumulator.
08E97D: C9E703          CMP #$03E7      ;Compare [Quotient] with 0x03E7(999).
08E980: 9003            BCC $E985       ;If it's less than 999 then branch over next instruction.
08E982: A9E703          LDA #$03E7      ;Load 0x03E7(999) into Accumulator.
08E985: 8589            STA $89         ;Store [Quotient] into {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E987: 8596            STA $96         ;Store [Quotient] into {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08E989: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E98A: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2 (04).
08E98B: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E98D: 859C            STA $9C         ;Store result into {TempVar:TimerLength}.
08E98F: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[CalculateMagicDefense]
;     If [Rnd(0..101)] < [Magic Evasion] Then
;         Magic Defense: MDEF + [Rnd(0..MDEF/16)]
;     Else
;         Magic Defense: (MEVA * [MDEF + {MDEF / 4}]) / 100
08E990: A965            LDA #$65        ;Load 0x65 into Accumulator.
08E992: 228438C0        JSR $C03884     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberRangeLong.
08E996: DDA7E1          CMP $E1A7,X     ;Compare result with [MAGIC_EVASION, X].
08E999: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E99B: B023            BCS $E9C0       ;If [Rnd(0..101)] is greater than [MAGIC_EVASION, X] then branch and calculate failure Magic Defense.
08E99D: BDA8E1          LDA $E1A8,X     ;Load [MAGIC_DEFENSE, X] into Accumulator.
08E9A0: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E9A1: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2 (04).
08E9A2: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2 (08).
08E9A3: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2 (16).
08E9A4: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9A6: 228438C0        JSR $C03884     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberRangeLong.
08E9AA: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9AC: 29FF00          AND #$00FF      ;Drop high byte.
08E9AF: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E9B0: 7DA8E1          ADC $E1A8,X     ;Add Accumulator to [MAGIC_DEFENSE, X].
08E9B3: C9E703          CMP #$03E7      ;Compare new Magic Defense with 0x03E7(999).
08E9B6: 9003            BCC $E9BB       ;Branch over next instruction if it is less than 0x03E7(999).
08E9B8: A9E703          LDA #$03E7      ;Load 0x03E7(999) into Accumulator.
08E9BB: 8594            STA $94         ;Store value into {TempVar:MagicDefense}.
08E9BD: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9BF: 60              RTS             ;Return.

08E9C0: BDA8E1          LDA $E1A8,X     ;Load [MAGIC_DEFENSE, X] into Accumulator.
08E9C3: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08E9C4: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2 (04).
08E9C5: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E9C6: 7DA8E1          ADC $E1A8,X     ;Add ([MAGIC_DEFENSE, X] / 4) to [MAGIC_DEFENSE, X].
08E9C9: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store value into {TempVar:MultiplicandB}.
08E9CB: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9CD: BDA7E1          LDA $E1A7,X     ;Load [MAGIC_EVASION, X] into Accumulator.
08E9D0: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store it in {TempVar:MultiplicandA}.
08E9D2: 223754C0        JSR $C05437     ;Jump to CalculateFailureValueLong.
08E9D6: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9D8: A5A4            LDA $A4         ;Load {TempVar:Result} into Accumulator.
08E9DA: 8594            STA $94         ;Store into {TempVar:MagicDefense}.
08E9DC: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9DE: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[ApplySpellDamage]
08E9DF: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9E1: A589            LDA $89         ;Load {TempVar:SpellPower} into Accumulator.
08E9E3: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08E9E4: E594            SBC $94         ;Subract {TempVar:MagicDefense} from {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E9E6: 1003            BPL $E9EB       ;If {TempSpellDamage} is greater than zero branch over next instruction.
08E9E8: A90100          LDA #$0001      ;Load 0x0001 into Accumulator.
08E9EB: D003            BNE $E9F0       ;Branch over next instruction if {TempVar:SpellPower} is not 0x00 or 0x01.
08E9ED: A90100          LDA #$0001      ;Load 0x0001 into Accumulator.
08E9F0: 8589            STA $89         ;Store Accumulator back into {TempVar:SpellPower}.
08E9F2: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
08E9F3: 7DF1E1          ADC $E1F1,X     ;Add {TempVar:SpellPower} to [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTS, X].
08E9F6: 9DF1E1          STA $E1F1,X     ;Store result back into [DAMAGE_HIT_POINTS, X].
08E9F9: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08E9FB: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[ClearStatusEffects]
;     Remedy and Revivifer call this.
;     Spell Level 8 removes all status effects except for Barrel and Ghost.
;     Spell Level >=4 removes all status effects except for Barrel and Ghost on a single target.
;     Otherwise it lowers every status effects timer by (SpellPower / 4).
;     It also always removes Pygmy no matter what.
08E9FC: BD91E1          LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
08E9FF: 29FD            AND #$FD        ;Remove Pygmy status.
08EA01: 9D91E1          STA $E191,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X].
08EA04: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel} into Accumulator.
08EA06: C908            CMP #$08        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} against 0x08.
08EA08: D01F            BNE $EA29       ;If the spell isn't level 8 then branch to LowerStatusEffectTimers.
08EA0A: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EA0C: 9DB2E1          STA $E1B2,X     ;Set [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION_01, X] to 0x01.
08EA0F: 9DB3E1          STA $E1B3,X     ;Set [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION_02, X] to 0x01.
08EA12: 9DB4E1          STA $E1B4,X     ;Set [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION_03, X] to 0x01.
08EA15: 9DB5E1          STA $E1B5,X     ;Set [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION_04, X] to 0x01.
08EA18: 9DB6E1          STA $E1B6,X     ;Set [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION_05, X] to 0x01.
08EA1B: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EA1D: BD90E1          LDA $E190,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS, X] into Accumulator.
08EA20: 290084          AND #$8400      ;Remove all effects except for Ghost and Barrel.
08EA23: 9D90E1          STA $E190,X     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS, X].
08EA26: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EA28: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LowerStatusEffectTimers]
08EA29: C904            CMP #$04        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} with 0x04.
08EA2B: 9009            BCC $EA36       ;Branch over target check if its less than 0x04.
08EA2D: B976E1          LDA $E176,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into Accumulator.
08EA30: C901            CMP #$01        ;Compare  [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] with 0x01.
08EA32: D002            BNE $EA36       ;Branch over next instruction if it's not equal.
08EA34: 80D4            BRA $EA0A       ;Branch up and remove all status effects.
08EA36: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EA38: A596            LDA $96         ;Load {TempVar:CastSpellPower} into Accumulator.
08EA3A: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08EA3B: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08EA3C: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EA3E: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:TimerReduction}.
08EA40: A905            LDA #$05        ;Load 0x05 into Accumulator.
08EA42: 85B2            STA $B2         ;Store 0x05 into {TempVar:LoopCounter}.
08EA44: BDB2E1          LDA $E1B2,X     ;Load [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION, X].
08EA47: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EA48: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:TimerReduction} from [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION, X].
08EA4A: B002            BCS $EA4E       ;Branch over next instruction if carry is still set.
08EA4C: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EA4E: D002            BNE $EA52       ;Branch over next instruction if [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION, X] is not zero.
08EA50: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EA52: 9DB2E1          STA $E1B2,X     ;Store 0x01 into [TIMER_STATUS_DURATION, X].
08EA55: E8              INX             ;Increment X.
08EA56: C6B2            DEC $B2         ;Decrement {TempVar:LoopCounter}.
08EA58: D0EA            BNE $EA44       ;If {TempVar:LoopCounter} is greater then zero, branch back up and alter the next timer.
08EA5A: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EA5C: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X. (Huh?)
08EA5E: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
08EA60: BDB1E1          LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
08EA63: 0904            ORA #$04        ;Set 'Partial Remedy Spell'.
08EA65: 9DB1E1          STA $E1B1,X     ;Store result back into [MISC_FLAGS, X].
08EA68: 60              RTS             ;Return.
