[SpellDefinition_DispelMagic]
;     Spell Level 8 removes all buff and debuff effects, all Saber uses, all Wall uses, and your current charge.
;     Spell Level >= 4 removes all buff and debuff effects, all Saber uses, all Wall uses, and your current charge on a single target.
;     Otherwise:
;     Lower every buff/debuff timer by ([SpellPower / 4] - [MagicDefense / 2])
;     Lower Saber spell uses by (ElementalLevel + 1) * 4.
;     Lower Wall uses by (ElementalLevel + 2).
;     If Wall Uses > 0 the "Can't Undo Wall!" message will be displayed.
;     
;     Of Note: Dispel Magic cannot remove Lucid Barrier or Barrel.
08EDCB: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EDCD: 9E9BE1          STZ $E19B,X     ;Store Zero into [CURRENT_CHARGE_LEVEL, X].
08EDD0: 20A0E8          JSR $E8A0       ;Jump to SetElementalResistanceValue.
08EDD3: 20C0E8          JSR $E8C0       ;Jump to CalculateSpellPowerAndTimer.
08EDD6: BDFAE1          LDA $E1FA,X     ;Load [7E\E1FA, X] into Accumulator.
08EDD9: 0904            ORA #$04        ;Set 0x04.
08EDDB: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store it back into [7E\E1FA, X].
08EDDE: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel} into Accumulator.
08EDE0: C908            CMP #$08        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} against 0x08.
08EDE2: D017            BNE $EDFB       ;If the spell isn't level 8 then branch to the next section.
08EDE4: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EDE6: 9DB7E1          STA $E1B7,X     ;Store 0x01 into [TIMER_ATTACK_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EDE9: 9DBAE1          STA $E1BA,X     ;Store 0x01 into [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EDEC: 9DBBE1          STA $E1BB,X     ;Store 0x01 into [TIMER_EVADE_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EDEF: 9DBCE1          STA $E1BC,X     ;Store 0x01 into [TIMER_DEFENSE_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EDF2: 9E9DE1          STZ $E19D,X     ;Store Zero [SABLE_SPELL_USES, X].
08EDF5: 9EB8E1          STZ $E1B8,X     ;Store Zero [WALL_USE_COUNTER, X].
08EDF8: 4C8DEE          JMP $EE8D       ;Jump out of routine.

08EDFB: C904            CMP #$04        ;Compare {TempVar:ElementalLevel} against 0x04.
08EDFD: 9009            BCC $EE08       ;If the spell is less than level 4 then branch to the next section.
08EDFF: B976E1          LDA $E176,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into Accumulator.
08EE02: C901            CMP #$01        ;Compare [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] against 0x01.
08EE04: D002            BNE $EE08       ;If there is more than one target branch over next instruction.
08EE06: 80DC            BRA $EDE4       ;Branch back up and do a full single target Dispel.

08EE08: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EE0A: BDA8E1          LDA $E1A8,X     ;Load [MAGIC_DEFENSE, X] into Accumulator.
08EE0D: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide by 2.
08EE0E: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:MagicDefense}.
08EE10: A596            LDA $96         ;Load {TempVar:CastSpellPower} into Accumulator.
08EE12: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide by 2.
08EE13: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide by 2 (04).
08EE14: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE15: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:MagicDefense} from {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08EE17: B003            BCS $EE1C       ;If result is greater than zero branch over next instruction.
08EE19: A90000          LDA #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Accumulator.
08EE1C: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EE1E: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:TimerReductionAmount}.

08EE20: BDB7E1          LDA $E1B7,X     ;Load [TIMER_ATTACK_BUFF_DURATION, X] into Accumulator.
08EE23: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE24: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:TimerReductionAmount} from [TIMER_ATTACK_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EE26: B002            BCS $EE2A       ;If result is greater then zero branch over next instruction.
08EE28: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE2A: D002            BNE $EE2E       ;If it's not zero, branch over next instruction.
08EE2C: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EE2E: 9DB7E1          STA $E1B7,X     ;Store result back into [TIMER_ATTACK_BUFF_DURATION, X].

08EE31: BDBAE1          LDA $E1BA,X     ;Load [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X] into Accumulator.
08EE34: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE35: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:TimerReductionAmount} from [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EE37: B002            BCS $EE3B       ;If result is greater then zero branch over next instruction.
08EE39: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE3B: D002            BNE $EE3F       ;If it's not zero, branch over next instruction.
08EE3D: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EE3F: 9DBAE1          STA $E1BA,X     ;Store result back into [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X].

08EE42: BDBBE1          LDA $E1BB,X     ;Load [TIMER_EVADE_BUFF_DURATION, X] into Accumulator.
08EE45: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE46: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:TimerReductionAmount} from [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EE48: B002            BCS $EE4C       ;If result is greater then zero branch over next instruction.
08EE4A: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE4C: D002            BNE $EE50       ;If it's not zero, branch over next instruction.
08EE4E: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EE50: 9DBBE1          STA $E1BB,X     ;Store result back into [TIMER_EVADE_BUFF_DURATION, X].

08EE53: BDBCE1          LDA $E1BC,X     ;Load [TIMER_DEFENSE_BUFF_DURATION, X] into Accumulator.
08EE56: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE57: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:TimerReductionAmount} from [TIMER_DEFENSE_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EE59: B002            BCS $EE5D       ;If result is greater then zero branch over next instruction.
08EE5B: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE5D: D002            BNE $EE61       ;If it's not zero, branch over next instruction.
08EE5F: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
08EE61: 9DBCE1          STA $E1BC,X     ;Store result back into [TIMER_DEFENSE_BUFF_DURATION, X].

08EE64: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EE66: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel} into Accumulator.
08EE68: 1A              INC A           ;Increment {TempVar:ElementalLevel}.
08EE69: 0A              ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
08EE6A: 0A              ASL A           ;Multiply By 2 (04).
08EE6B: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:CounterReductionAmount}.

08EE6D: BD9DE1          LDA $E19D,X     ;Load [SABLE_SPELL_USES, X] into Accumulator.
08EE70: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE71: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:CounterReductionAmount} from [SABLE_SPELL_USES, X].
08EE73: 1002            BPL $EE77       ;If result is positive then branch over next instruction.
08EE75: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE77: 9D9DE1          STA $E19D,X     ;Store result into [SABLE_SPELL_USES, X].

08EE7A: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalLevel} into Accumulator.
08EE7C: 1A              INC A           ;Increment {TempVar:ElementalLevel}.
08EE7D: 1A              INC A           ;Increment {TempVar:ElementalLevel}.
08EE7E: 85A4            STA $A4         ;Store result into {TempVar:CounterReductionAmount}.

08EE80: BDB8E1          LDA $E1B8,X     ;Load [WALL_USE_COUNTER, X] into Accumulator.
08EE83: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
08EE84: E5A4            SBC $A4         ;Subtract {TempVar:CounterReductionAmount} from [WALL_USE_COUNTER, X].
08EE86: 1002            BPL $EE8A       ;If result is positive then branch over next instruction.
08EE88: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EE8A: 9DB8E1          STA $E1B8,X     ;Store result back into [WALL_USE_COUNTER, X].

08EE8D: BDB8E1          LDA $E1B8,X     ;Load [WALL_USE_COUNTER, X] into Accumulator.
08EE90: D008            BNE $EE9A       ;If it's not zero branch to next section.
08EE92: BDB1E1          LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
08EE95: 29BF            AND #$BF        ;Remove 'Wall'.
08EE97: 9DB1E1          STA $E1B1,X     ;Store back into [MISC_FLAGS, X].

08EE9A: BD9DE1          LDA $E19D,X     ;Load [SABLE_SPELL_USES, X] into Accumulator.
08EE9D: D00C            BNE $EEAB       ;If it's not zero then branch to next section.
08EE9F: BDFAE1          LDA $E1FA,X     ;Load  [7E\E1FA, X] into Accumulator.
08EEA2: 0904            ORA #$04        ;Set 0x04.
08EEA4: 9DFAE1          STA $E1FA,X     ;Store back into [7E\E1FA, X].
08EEA7: 223045C0        JSR $C04530     ;Jump to MasterCharacterSetup.

08EEAB: BDB1E1          LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
08EEAE: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Test for 'Wall'
08EEB0: F004            BEQ $EEB6       ;If it's not set branch to end of routine.
08EEB2: 22B65BC0        JSR $C05BB6     ;Jump to PumpDispelFailureMessageToRAM.
08EEB6: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[SpellDefinition_LucidBarrier]
08EEB7: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EEB9: A904            LDA #$04        ;Load 0x04 into Accumulator.
08EEBB: 858F            STA $8F         ;Store it into {TempVar:WeaknessVar}.
08EEBD: 20C0E8          JSR $E8C0       ;Jump to CalculateSpellPowerAndTimer.
08EEC0: BDB1E1          LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
08EEC3: 0910            ORA #$10        ;Set 'Lucid Barrier'.
08EEC5: 9DB1E1          STA $E1B1,X     ;Store back into [MISC_FLAGS, X].
08EEC8: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EECA: BDA5E1          LDA $E1A5,X     ;Load [DEFENSE, X] into Accumulator.
08EECD: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08EECE: 8F044200        STA $004204     ;Store result into [Dividend].
08EED2: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EED4: B976E1          LDA $E176,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into Accumulator.
08EED7: 8F064200        STA $004206     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_TARGET_COUNT, Y] into [Divisor B].
08EEDB: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EEDD: BD84E1          LDA $E184,X     ;Load [MAX_HIT_POINTS, X] into Accumulator.
08EEE0: C589            CMP $89         ;Compare [MAX_HIT_POINTS, X] to {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08EEE2: B002            BCS $EEE6       ;If its greater then branch over next instruction.
08EEE4: 8589            STA $89         ;Store [MAX_HIT_POINTS, X] into {TempVar:CastSpellPower}.
08EEE6: A589            LDA $89         ;Load {TempVar:CastSpellPower} into Accumulator.
08EEE8: 9DBDE1          STA $E1BD,X     ;Store result into [LUCID_BARRIER_HIT_POINTS, X]
08EEEB: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EEED: AF144200        LDA $004214     ;Load [Quotient] into Accumulator.
08EEF1: 9DBFE1          STA $E1BF,X     ;Store [Quotient] into [LUCID_BARRIER_DEFENSE, X].
08EEF4: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[SpellDefinition_LunarMagic]
08EEF5: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EEF7: 20A0E8          JSR $E8A0       ;Jump to SetElementalResistanceValue.
08EEFA: 20C0E8          JSR $E8C0       ;Jump to CalculateSpellPowerAndTimer.
08EEFD: A908            LDA #$08        ;Load 0x08 into Accumulator.
08EEFF: 228438C0        JSR $C03884     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberRangeLong.
08EF03: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EF05: 290F00          AND #$000F      ;Drop everything but 0x000F. (Get a random number between 0x00 and 0x07)
08EF08: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EF0A: 48              PHA             ;Push Accumulator.
08EF0B: 22A45BC0        JSR $C05BA4     ;Jump to PumpLunarMagicMessage.
08EF0F: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
08EF11: EB              XBA             ;Exchange A & B Accumulators.
08EF12: 68              PLA             ;Pull Accumulator. (Gets our random number back off the stack)
08EF13: 0A              ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
08EF14: AA              TAX             ;Transfer Accumulator to Register X.
08EF15: FC19EF          JSR ($EF19,X)   ;Jump To PointerTable_LunarMagicEffects, X.
08EF18: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[PointerTable_LunarMagicEffects]
08EF19: 2BEF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_RestorePartyEnemyHP.
08EF1B: 48EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_BuffTargetedEnemy.
08EF1D: 5EEF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_DebuffTargetedEnemy.
08EF1F: 74EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_ConfuseAll.
08EF21: 8AEF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_MoogleAll.
08EF23: A0EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_PygmyParty.
08EF25: B6EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_TransformAllEnemies.
08EF27: D0EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_KnockOutAll.
08EF29: E6EF                            ;Pointer to LunarMagicEffect_HalfCasterMaxMP (Dummied Out).

[LunarMagicEffect_RestorePartyEnemyHP]
08EF2B: A0000A          LDY $0A00       ;Load 0x0A00 into Register Y. (Max Character Offset in Combat zones)
08EF2E: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:MaxCharacterOffset}.
08EF30: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:MaxCharacterOffset} into Register Y.
08EF32: B991E1          LDA $E191,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y] into Accumulator.
08EF35: 300A            BMI $EF41       ;If current target has 'Ghost' status then branch to the next character offset.
08EF37: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EF39: B984E1          LDA $E184,Y     ;Load [MAX_HIT_POINTS, Y] into Accumulator.
08EF3C: 99F3E1          STA $E1F3,Y     ;Store [MAX_HIT_POINTS, Y] into [RESTORE_HIT_POINTS, Y].
08EF3F: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
08EF41: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:MaxCharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF43: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:MaxCharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF45: 10E9            BPL $EF30       ;If {TempVar:MaxCharacterOffset} > 0 then branch to the next character offset.
08EF47: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_BuffTargetedEnemy]
08EF48: A685            LDX $85         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTarget} into Register X.
08EF4A: A59C            LDA $9C         ;Load {TempVar:TimerLength} into Accumulator.
08EF4C: 9DB7E1          STA $E1B7,X     ;Store {TempVar:TimerLength} into [TIMER_ATTACK_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EF4F: 9DBAE1          STA $E1BA,X     ;Store {TempVar:TimerLength} into [TIMER_HIT_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EF52: 9DBBE1          STA $E1BB,X     ;Store {TempVar:TimerLength} into [TIMER_EVADE_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EF55: 9DBCE1          STA $E1BC,X     ;Store {TempVar:TimerLength} into [TIMER_DEFENSE_BUFF_DURATION, X].
08EF58: A955            LDA #$55        ;Load 0x55 into Accumulator. ('Attack Up', 'Hit Up', 'Evade Up', 'Defense Up').
08EF5A: 9DB0E1          STA $E1B0,X     ;Store 0x55 into [BUFF_EFFECTS, X].
08EF5D: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_DebuffTargetedEnemy]

[LunarMagicEffect_ConfuseAll]
08EF74: A0000A          LDY #$0A00      ;Load 0x0A00 into Register Y. (Max Character Offset in Combat zones)
08EF77: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:CharacterOffset}.
08EF79: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset} into Register Y.
08EF7B: B990E1          LDA $E190,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_01, Y] into Accumulator.
08EF7E: 0980            ORA #$80        ;Set 'Confusion'.
08EF80: 9990E1          STA $E190,Y     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_01, Y].
08EF83: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF85: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF87: 10F0            BPL $EF79       ;If positive branch back and Confuse the next character.
08EF89: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_MoogleAll]
;     All enemies are inherently immune to Moogle. Why does this try to inflict them anyway?

08EF8D: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:CharacterOffset}.

08EF91: B991E1          LDA $E191,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y] into Accumulator.
08EF94: 0910            ORA #$10        ;Set 'Moogle'.
08EF96: 9991E1          STA $E191,Y     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y].
08EF99: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF9B: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EF9D: 10F0            BPL $EF8F       ;If positive branch back and Moogle the next character.
08EF9F: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_PygmyParty]

08EFA7: B991E1          LDA $E191,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y] into Accumulator.
08EFAA: 0902            ORA #$02        ;Set 'Pygmy'.
08EFAC: 9991E1          STA $E191,Y     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y].
08EFAF: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.

[LunarMagicEffect_TransformAllEnemies]
08EFB6: A00006          LDY #$0600      ;Load 0x0600 into Register Y. (First Enemy Offset)
08EFB9: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:CharacterOffset}.
08EFBB: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset} into Register Y.
08EFBD: B991E1          LDA $E191,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y] into Accumulator.
08EFC0: 0908            ORA #$08        ;Set 'Transform'.
08EFC2: 9991E1          STA $E191,Y     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, Y].
08EFC5: E6CD            INC $CD         ;Increment {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EFC7: E6CD            INC $CD         ;Increment {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EFC9: A5CD            LDA $CD         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)} into Accumulator.
08EFCB: C90C            CMP #$0C        ;Compare {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)} against 0C. (Last valid offset = 0A).
08EFCD: D0EC            BNE $EFBB       ;If its not equal branch back and Transform next enemy.
08EFCF: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_KnockOutAll]
08EFD0: A0000A          LDY #$0A00      ;Load 0x0A00 into Register Y. (Max Character Offset in Combat zones)
08EFD3: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:CharacterOffset}.
08EFD5: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:CharacterOffset} into Register Y.
08EFD7: B990E1          LDA $E190,Y     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_01, Y] into Accumulator.
08EFDA: 0910            ORA #$10        ;Set 'Knocked Out'.
08EFDC: 9990E1          STA $E190,Y     ;Store result back into [STATUS_EFFECTS_01, Y].
08EFDF: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EFE1: C6CD            DEC $CD         ;Decrement {TempVar:CharacterOffset (High)}.
08EFE3: 10F0            BPL $EFD5       ;If positive branch back and knockout the next character.
08EFE5: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LunarMagicEffect_HalfCasterMaxMP (Dummied Out)]
;     Message for this is "MP recovered!". Comically misleading.
;     This effect can't happen anyway since its outside the random numbers range.
;     It's also extremely temporary as most actions cause a character stat refresh, setting your stats back to normal.
;     Also it doesn't touch current MP so you'll end up with something like 99/49 MP.
;
;     I assume it was supposed to restore 50% of your max MP, but the code is unfinished.
08EFE6: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
08EFE8: B987E1          LDA $E187,Y     ;Load [MAX_MANA_POINTS, Y] into Accumulator.
08EFEB: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide By 2.
08EFEC: 9987E1          STA $E187,Y     ;Store result back into [MAX_MANA_POINTS, Y].
08EFEF: 60              RTS             ;Return.
